#### 13.4.14 Recommended Change Inventory (`Section 14` — `RECOMMENDED`)

| Change ID | Category | Component | Recommended Enhancement | Rationale | Priority |
|:---------:|:---------|:----------|:------------------------|:----------|:--------:|
| `SEP-REC-01` | `Authorization & RBAC` | `TenantRolePermissionOverrides` | Allow a Customer `TenantOwner` to customize permissions for their own company's `Manager` / `WarehouseStaff` roles via a tenant-scoped override table (`TenantRolePermissions`) while keeping `dbo.RolePermissions` as immutable platform defaults. | Gives enterprise customers custom role flexibility without mutating global `RolePermissions` across tenants. | `P1` |
| `SEP-REC-02` | `Support & Impersonation` | Dual-Control Customer Consent Toggle (`AllowSupportAccess`) | Add `AllowSupportAccess` (`bool`, default `true` with instant revoke) on `SaasTenants` so enterprise tenants with strict compliance can require explicit opt-in before a `SupportAdmin` starts a read-only impersonation session. | Strengthens B2B enterprise trust and PDPA (`Nghị định 13/2023/NĐ-CP`) compliance. | `P1` |
| `SEP-REC-03` | `Authentication` | Mandatory TOTP MFA Enforcement for `PLATFORM` Accounts | Require `TotpEnrollmentStatus == "ACTIVE"` for `PlatformOwner`, `PlatformAdmin`, `BillingAdmin`, and `SupportAdmin` before issuing a privileged platform session or starting support impersonation. | Protects the "keys to the kingdom" (`SaaS Control Center`) against credential stuffing. | `P1` |
| `SEP-REC-04` | `Quota & Usage Metering` | Daily Tenant Usage Metering Snapshot (`TenantUsageSnapshots`) | Record daily tenant usage metrics (`ActiveWarehouses`, `ActiveUsers`, `ActiveSkus`, `MonthlyDispatchedOrders`, `StorageVolumeCbm`, `OutboxEventsDispatched`) for billing trend charts in `/platform/quotas`. | Prepares for usage-based B2B SaaS billing (`[SAAS-004]`). | `P2` |
| `SEP-REC-05` | `Integrations` | Platform Carrier Sandbox vs Production Gateway Switch | Allow `PlatformAdmin` to view which tenants are running on `DEV_SANDBOX` (`ShopId 235512`) vs `PRODUCTION` carrier gateways in `/platform/integrations` without exposing raw partner tokens (`0c06****12be`). | Prevents accidental production vs sandbox misconfiguration across onboarding merchants. | `P2` |
| `SEP-REC-06` | `Frontend Shell` | Active Impersonation Top Ribbon in `CustomerWmsLayout` | Render a high-contrast amber/red sticky top ribbon inside `CustomerWmsLayout` whenever `sessionStorage.getItem('impersonation_session_code')` is active, showing `TargetTenantCode`, `SupportTicketCode`, countdown timer, and `"Kết thúc phiên hỗ trợ"` button. | Prevents a support engineer from forgetting they are viewing a customer tenant and provides visual proof for Chrome audits. | `P1` |
| `SEP-REC-07` | `Customer Settings` | Dedicated `/settings` Workspace Group in Customer Portal | Rename the Customer navigation group from `"Quản trị"` to `"Quản trị Kho & Cài đặt"` containing `/approvals`, `/cost-valuation`, `/permissions` (`Nhân sự & Phân quyền Kho`), `/saas-billing` (`Gói thuê bao của công ty`), and `/onboarding-wizard` (`Thiết lập kho ban đầu`). | Clarifies that these screens belong to the Customer Company's own warehouse administration, not Platform Control Plane. | `P1` |
| `SEP-REC-08` | `Platform Settings` | Global Announcement / Maintenance Banner | Allow `PlatformAdmin` in `/platform/settings` to publish a scheduled maintenance or carrier-outage notice banner to all Customer WMS tenants. | Essential operational tool for SaaS incident communication. | `P2` |

#### 13.4.17 Risks, Dependencies & Backward-Compatibility Safeguards (`Section 17`)

| Risk ID | Risk Description | Severity | Mitigation Strategy |
|:-------:|:-----------------|:--------:|:--------------------|
| `RSK-SEP-01` | **Breaking Existing `562` `ERP.Api.Tests` that Authenticate as `qa_admin` (`TenantId = 1`, `Role = "Admin"`):** Many existing integration tests log in as `qa_admin` and call both warehouse endpoints (`/api/v1/import-receipts`, `/api/v1/shipments`) and SaaS governance endpoints (`/api/v1/permissions/saas/*`, `/api/v1/saas-billing/*`). | `High` | Keep `qa_admin` in `Testing` / seeded QA environment or issue a dual-compatible test claim / seed a dedicated `platform_admin` account while preserving legacy route aliases on `PermissionsController` for `qa_admin` (`IsSystemBypass` or `PlatformOwner` in test harness), and write dedicated strict boundary tests in `PlatformSeparationSecurityTests.cs` using distinct `customer_owner_t2` (`CUSTOMER_TENANT`) and `platform_ops_admin` (`PLATFORM`) principals. |
| `RSK-SEP-02` | **Locking Out Customer Admins from Managing Their Own Company Users:** If `POST /api/v1/permissions/roles/{roleId}/grants` is moved to Platform Admin only, Customer Admins still need to create/lock/unlock their own company's users and assign warehouse scopes (`/api/v1/users/{userId}/warehouse-access`). | `Medium` | Keep tenant user management (`GET /api/v1/permissions/users`, `AccountSecurityController` lock/unlock/reset/TOTP for same-tenant users, and `UserWarehouseAccessController`) in Customer Portal, while blocking cross-tenant user access and global `RolePermissions` table mutation. |
| `RSK-SEP-03` | **Refresh Token Cookie Collision on `localhost` During Dual-Portal Testing:** Browser sends `erp_refresh` (`Path=/`) to all `/api/v1/*` routes on the same host. | `Medium` | Use a distinct cookie name `erp_platform_refresh` and distinct storage key `platform_access_token` when logging in via `/api/v1/platform/auth/login` (`/platform/login`). |
| `RSK-SEP-04` | **Page & Module LOC Ceiling (`pagesGt500 == 0`, `modsGt400 == 0`):** Adding `/platform/*` pages or refactoring `Permissions.tsx` (`398 LOC`) and `SaasBilling.tsx` (`398 LOC`) must not push any page over `500 LOC` or any module over `400 LOC`. | `Low` | Create modular `< 300 LOC` page files under `frontend/src/pages/platform/` and `frontend/src/modules/platform/`, reducing the LOC of `Permissions.tsx` and `SaasBilling.tsx`. |

#### 13.4.19 Multi-Agent Review Findings Synthesis (`Section 19`)
1. **`AGENT MAIN` (`AGENT-00` — Principal Architect & Forensic Reviewer):**
   - Verified via direct `read_file` across `Program.cs`, `ErpKhoDbContext.cs`, `AuthController.cs`, `TokenService.cs`, `PermissionsController.cs`, `SaasBillingController.cs`, `AccountSecurityController.cs`, `SupportImpersonationMiddleware.cs`, `PermissionOperationsService.cs`, `WmsGovernanceOperationsService.cs`, `AppRoutes.tsx`, `MainLayout.tsx`, `Permissions.tsx`, `SaasBilling.tsx`, and `OnboardingWizard.tsx`.
   - Confirmed that **Option A + Option B Hybrid** (`Two Application Shells in One Frontend Workspace + Shared Backend with /api/v1/platform/* Route Group & SecurityDomain Isolation`) delivers 100% of the functional and security separation goals with the lowest regression risk and zero duplication of the 9D Inventory Engine.
2. **`AGENT 1` (`Backend Architecture & API Boundary Assessment`):**
   - Confirmed that `34/43` Controllers are pure `CUSTOMER_API`, `4/43` (`UserWarehouseAccess`, `Reports`, `Approvals`, `DataImports`/`ImportTemplates`) are `CUSTOMER_API` with tenant/warehouse guards, and only **`5/43` Controllers** (`AuthController`, `PermissionsController`, `SaasBillingController`, `AccountSecurityController`, `ShipmentsController` outbox/carrier health) require splitting or `/api/v1/platform/*` counterparts.
   - Pinpointed the exact lines in `PermissionOperationsService.cs` (`L209`, `L261`, `L470-498`, `L813-855`, `L1252-1300`) where Customer `Admin`/`Manager` permissions currently overlap with Platform Control Plane actions.
3. **`AGENT 2` (`Frontend Shell, Route & Component Boundary Assessment`):**
   - Confirmed that `37/46` canonical routes (`R004`–`R014`, `R016`–`R022`, `R024`, `R026`–`R038`, `R044`–`R046`) belong 100% to `CUSTOMER_PORTAL` without change; `1/46` (`R002` `/register`) is `SHARED_PUBLIC`; `2/46` (`R039`, `R041` embedded cards in `Permissions.tsx`) move 100% to `PLATFORM_PORTAL`; and `6/46` (`R001` `/login`, `R003` `/`, `R015` `/shipments`, `R023` `/inventory-reconciliation`, `R025` `/permissions`, `R040` support sessions, `R042` `/onboarding-wizard`, `R043` `/saas-billing`) require clean splitting between `CustomerWmsLayout` and `PlatformLayout`.
4. **`AGENT 3` (`Independent QA & Security Boundary Assessment`):**
   - Highlighted 4 P0 security boundary test vectors (`SEP-SEC-01` through `SEP-SEC-04`) that must be enforced in Phase 1–2 and verified in Phase 5 (`TC-SEP-01` Customer token calling `/api/v1/platform/*` $\rightarrow$ `403`, `TC-SEP-02` Platform token calling `/api/v1/import-receipts` without impersonation $\rightarrow$ `403`, `TC-SEP-03` Support Impersonation token calling `POST` mutation $\rightarrow$ `403 IMPERSONATION_READ_ONLY_ENFORCED`, `TC-SEP-04` Customer Admin calling `POST /api/v1/permissions/roles/{id}/grants` or `enforce-quota` $\rightarrow$ `403`).

#### 13.4.20 Project Owner Decisions Required Before Phase 1 (`Section 20` — `[WF-003]` `PENDING DECISION`)
Per Section XIII (`PHASE GATE RULE`) and `[WF-003]`, **no code or database changes have been made in Phase 0**. Before proceeding to **Phase 1 (`Identity, Security Domain, RBAC & Additive DB Migration #65`)**, please review and confirm the following **5 Architectural Decisions**:

1. **`DECISION-SEP-01` — Target Frontend Separation Architecture (`Option A + Option B Hybrid` Recommended):**
   - **Recommended:** Implement **Two Application Shells (`CustomerWmsLayout` vs `PlatformControlCenterLayout`)** inside the existing `frontend/` workspace supporting **both** path-based routing (`/platform/*` vs `/*` for local dev, CI, and Live Chrome audits) **and** hostname-based routing (`admin.example.vn` vs `app.example.vn` via `resolvePortalMode()`).
   - *Question:* Do you approve **Option A + Option B Hybrid**, or do you prefer splitting `frontend/` into two completely separate `package.json` apps right now?
2. **`DECISION-SEP-02` — Target Backend API Separation (`Shared ERP.Api with /api/v1/platform/* Route Group` Recommended):**
   - **Recommended:** Keep a single `ERP.Api` modular monolith process and introduce dedicated `/api/v1/platform/*` controllers guarded by `[PlatformAuthorize]` (`SecurityDomain == "PLATFORM"`), while locking `/api/v1/*` Customer operational endpoints with `[CustomerTenantAuthorize]`.
   - *Question:* Do you approve keeping a single `ERP.Api` host with `/api/v1/platform/*` vs `/api/v1/*` separation (rather than spinning up a second backend `.csproj` process `Option C`)?
3. **`DECISION-SEP-03` — Platform Admin vs Customer Admin Role & Permission Boundary:**
   - **Recommended:**
     + **Platform Roles (`SecurityDomain = "PLATFORM"`):** `PlatformOwner`, `PlatformAdmin`, `BillingAdmin`, `SupportAdmin`, `SecurityAuditor`, `PlatformReadOnly`.
     + **Customer Roles (`SecurityDomain = "CUSTOMER_TENANT"`):** `TenantOwner` (or `Admin` scoped strictly to `CUSTOMER_TENANT`), `Manager`, `Accountant`, `WarehouseStaff`, `Viewer`.
     + Lock global `dbo.Roles` and `dbo.RolePermissions` so Customer Admins **cannot** mutate global role permission grants (`SEP-SEC-02`) or override their own `MonthlyOrderQuota` (`SEP-SEC-03`).
   - *Question:* Do you approve locking global `RolePermissions` and quota overrides exclusively to Platform Admins?
4. **`DECISION-SEP-04` — Direct Warehouse Access Policy for Platform Staff (`Impersonation-Only` Recommended):**
   - **Recommended:** Platform Admin accounts (`SecurityDomain = "PLATFORM"`) **cannot** directly view or mutate a Customer Tenant's warehouse data using their Platform JWT alone; to view a customer's screens in Customer WMS Portal, they **must** initiate an audited, ticket-linked `SupportImpersonationSession` (`ReadOnlyScope = true` by default, TTL `15–240` minutes), and Customer `TenantOwner` can view/revoke active support sessions at any time.
   - *Question:* Do you approve enforcing mandatory read-only Support Impersonation for all Platform-to-Customer access?
5. **`DECISION-SEP-05` — Additive Database Migration `#65` (`dbo.SaasTenants` + `SecurityDomain` Columns):**
   - **Recommended:** Authorize **1 additive EF Core migration (`#65`)** in Phase 1 to create `dbo.SaasTenants` (`TenantId`, `TenantCode`, `CompanyName`, `TaxCode`, `OwnerEmail`, `LifecycleStatus`, `SuspensionReason`, `RowVersion`) and add `SecurityDomain` (`'PLATFORM' | 'CUSTOMER_TENANT'`) to `Users`, `Roles`, and `AuditLogs`.
   - *Question:* Do you approve creating additive Migration `#65` when starting Phase 1?

#### 13.15.3 `CA-QA-ADVERSARIAL-001` Independent 5-Round Adversarial QA Governance (`Sections A → Q`)

Per `CA-QA-ADVERSARIAL-001`, independent adversarial verification is structured across **Sections `A → Q`** and persisted in `artifacts/live-browser-audit/current/`:
